```python
def test_requeue_policy_is_declared_per_block():
    """Which aborts owe a datum is a task's judgement, not the framework's.

    A block training an animal to hold fixation may treat a fixation break as a real
    error that pays its debt; a block measuring a psychometric function must not.
    """
    strict = Block(
        name="strict",
        conditions=[Condition(name="a", values={}, target=1)],
        requeue_on=frozenset(),
    )
    scheduler = Scheduler(blocks=[strict], seed=1)
    scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK)

    assert scheduler.requeued == []
```

with

```python
def test_requeue_policy_is_declared_per_block():
    """Which aborts owe a datum is a task's judgement, not the framework's.

    A block training an animal to hold fixation may treat a fixation break as a real
    error that pays its debt; a block measuring a psychometric function must not. The
    block's table replaces its structure's (TSK-26).
    """
    strict = Block(
        name="strict",
        conditions=[Condition(name="a", values={}, target=1)],
        counting=Counting(after_stimulus=OWED, outcomes={"fixation_break": COUNTED}),
    )
    scheduler = Scheduler(blocks=[strict], seed=1, counting=Counting(after_stimulus=OWED))
    scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK)

    assert scheduler.requeued == []
    assert scheduler.owed("a") == 0


# --- the counting table (engine spec §16.1-16.2; engine build C2a) ---------------------


def _catch(**table) -> Scheduler:
    """One condition owing three trials, counted by a structure's table built from `table`."""
    block = Block(name="catch", conditions=[Condition("catch", {}, target=3)])
    return Scheduler([block], seed=1, counting=Counting(**table))


def test_a_catch_condition_meets_its_target_on_correct_rejections():
    """§16.1; TSK-27. `Counting.RESPONDED` left out correct rejections and false alarms, so ten
    correct rejections left a catch condition owing 3, never repeated, and its block never ended
    (the engine C2a plan's first defect)."""
    scheduler = _catch(after_stimulus=OWED, withholding=True)

    for outcome in (Outcome.CORRECT_REJECT, Outcome.FALSE_ALARM, Outcome.CORRECT_REJECT):
        assert scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, outcome) == COUNTED

    assert scheduler.finished
    assert scheduler.requeued == []


@pytest.mark.parametrize("after", [SPENT, OWED])
def test_a_target_break_is_spent_or_owed_as_the_structure_declares(after):
    """§16.2; the PI, 2026-10-11. `REQUEUED` repeated a target break and a blink break whatever a
    task said (the plan's second defect). Spent pays the debt and is marked an abort; owed pays
    nothing and comes back."""
    scheduler = _catch(after_stimulus=after)

    for outcome in (Outcome.TARGET_BREAK, Outcome.BLINK_BREAK):
        assert scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, outcome) == after

    counts = scheduler.counts("catch")
    if after == SPENT:
        assert (counts.spent, counts.completed, scheduler.owed("catch")) == (2, 0, 1)
        assert scheduler.requeued == []
    else:
        assert (counts.spent, scheduler.owed("catch")) == (0, 3)
        assert scheduler.requeued == ["catch", "catch"]


def test_a_break_before_the_stimulus_appeared_is_owed_even_where_breaks_are_spent():
    """§16.1: "Not counted: trials that end before the decision". A long blink in the fixation hold
    presented nothing (`shown` False: `taskd._appeared`)."""
    scheduler = _catch(after_stimulus=SPENT)

    assert scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.BLINK_BREAK, shown=False) == OWED
    assert scheduler.owed("catch") == 3 and scheduler.requeued == ["catch"]


def test_a_spent_trial_pays_its_debt_and_is_no_answer_for_the_criterion():
    """§16.2: spent is "counted as presented and marked an abort", so a criterion judged on the
    answers does not see it."""
    block = Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("a", {}, target=100)], criterion=(0.5, 2))
    scheduler = Scheduler([block], seed=1, counting=Counting(after_stimulus=SPENT))

    for outcome in (Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.TARGET_BREAK):
        scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, outcome)

    assert scheduler.owed("a") == 98
    assert not scheduler.finished, "one answer is not a window of two"
    scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT)
    assert scheduler.finished, "two correct answers are"


def test_a_trial_at_the_longest_trial_is_owed_and_in_a_flat_run_pays():
    """XC-287; the PI, 2026-10-11: a trial that reaches the cap is a missed acquisition, repeated
    like a fault. `None` neither counted nor was requeued, so a flat run ran N trials plus its hangs
    (the plan's fourth defect); in a flat run every trial pays, this one too."""
    owed = _catch(after_stimulus=SPENT)
    flat = Scheduler(
        [Block(name="session", conditions=[Condition("session", {}, target=2)],
               counting=EVERY_TRIAL)],
        seed=1,
    )

    assert owed.record(owed.next_trial().name, None) == OWED
    assert owed.requeued == ["catch"] and owed.owed("catch") == 3
    assert flat.record(flat.next_trial().name, None) == SPENT
    assert flat.owed("session") == 1


def test_an_outcome_each_structure_declares_is_refused_with_no_table_to_read():
    """The PI, 2026-10-11: no default stands in for a declaration. What every structure shares
    still answers without one."""
    scheduler = Scheduler([_block()], seed=1)

    assert scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK) == OWED
    assert scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT) == COUNTED
    assert scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT, shown=False) == COUNTED
    assert scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, None) == OWED
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="target_break ended a trial of block 'main'"):
        scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.TARGET_BREAK)


def test_a_wrong_answer_under_until_n_correct_pays_nothing_and_is_not_shown_again():
    """The PI, 2026-08-31: "wrong choice not" re-queued; §16.2: "Not repeated: wrong targets". A
    wrong answer under "run until ten correct" leaves the debt standing, and its condition comes
    back when the block's order draws it, never placed as a repeat."""
    block = Block(name="main", conditions=[Condition(n, {}, target=2) for n in "ab"],
                  counting=CORRECT_ONLY)
    scheduler = Scheduler([block], seed=1)
    wrong = scheduler.next_trial().name

    assert scheduler.record(wrong, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET) == UNPAID
    assert scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT_REJECT) == COUNTED
    assert scheduler.requeued == [] and scheduler.owed(wrong) == 2


def test_a_correct_rejection_is_right_for_the_criterion_and_the_correct_count():
    """`codes.py`: "A correct rejection *is* a correct trial", and the PI's rollup of 2026-09-26
    counts both: a criterion over a block of catch trials is met on correct rejections."""
    block = Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("catch", {}, target=100)], criterion=(1.0, 2))
    scheduler = Scheduler([block], seed=1, counting=Counting(after_stimulus=OWED, withholding=True))

    for _ in range(2):
        scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT_REJECT)

    assert scheduler.finished and scheduler.counts("catch").correct == 2
```

```python
def test_each_trial_row_says_what_it_did_to_its_count_and_whether_its_stimulus_appeared(tmp_path):
    """Engine spec §16.1-16.2, §16.4: a block counted by the reference task's own table -- no
    response a miss, counted; a fixation break owed -- and every row says which, and whether the
    target appeared (the trial entered `stim_on`, the state its table names)."""
    block = Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("c", {}, target=30)])
    session = _session(_spec(tmp_path, trials=400, blocks=[block]))

    session.run()

    rows = _trial_rows(session)
    said = {(row["outcome"], row["counting"], row["shown"]) for row in rows}
    assert ("correct", "counted", True) in said
    assert all(row["counting"] == "counted" for row in rows if row["outcome"] == "no_response")
    assert all((row["counting"], row["shown"]) == ("owed", False)
               for row in rows if row["outcome"] in ("no_fixation", "fixation_break"))
    assert sum(row["counting"] == "counted" for row in rows) == 30


def test_whether_a_trials_stimulus_appeared_is_read_from_the_state_its_table_names():
    """The PI, 2026-10-13, "Task names it, required": "A cued task names the cue step." A trial that
    ended in its cue's state presented something, whatever its reaction time's state; one whose
    table names a later state had not. A structure with no table -- a test's, since the load-time
    check refuses one in a task -- counts every trial as shown."""
    from wl_xcon import taskd

    cue = Trial(start="cue", states=[State("cue", go=[On(After(0.1), "go")]),
                                     State("go", go=[On(After(0.1), Outcome.CORRECT)])],
                counting=Counting(after_stimulus=OWED, appears="cue"))
    later = dataclasses.replace(cue, counting=Counting(after_stimulus=OWED, appears="go"))
    broke_in_cue = SimpleNamespace(visited=("cue",))

    assert taskd._appeared(cue, broke_in_cue) and not taskd._appeared(later, broke_in_cue)
    assert taskd._appeared(dataclasses.replace(cue, counting=None), broke_in_cue)
```

`wl_xcon/scheduler.py`: replace

```python
class Counting:
    """Which outcomes pay a condition's debt. **Declared per block** (PI, 2026-08-31),
    because the rule is task-specific and no single answer is right.

    "Run until ten correct" and "show the array a hundred times regardless of
    accuracy" are both ordinary requirements, and they disagree about every trial
    that is not correct. A scheduler that picked one would silently impose an
    experimental design on every task in the lab.
    """

    #: The animal chose. Wrong is still a datum about the condition.
    RESPONDED = frozenset(
        {
            Outcome.CORRECT,
            Outcome.WRONG_TARGET,
            Outcome.EARLY_RESPONSE,
            Outcome.LATE_RESPONSE,
            Outcome.EARLY_ERROR,
            Outcome.LATE_ERROR,
        }
    )

    #: Only a correct trial pays. An error leaves the debt standing.
    CORRECT_ONLY = frozenset({Outcome.CORRECT})

    #: The stimulus was shown, however the trial ended. Excludes the failures that
    #: happen *before* onset -- an animal that never fixated was never presented
    #: with anything, which is the distinction this exists to draw.
    PRESENTED = RESPONDED | {
        Outcome.NO_RESPONSE,
        Outcome.TARGET_BREAK,
        Outcome.CATCH_BREAK,
    }

    #: Everything, including trials the animal never engaged with.
    EVERY_TRIAL = frozenset(Outcome)


#: Retained for readers of older commits; `Counting.RESPONDED` is the name now.
COMPLETED = Counting.RESPONDED

#: The default requeue policy: outcomes where the condition still owes a datum, so
#: the trial comes back. Two families.
#:
#: **The animal failed to engage** rather than choosing wrongly -- it did not answer,
#: so nothing was measured.
#:
#: **The rig failed**, which is not the animal's doing at all. These were added on
#: 2026-09-01 with the outcomes themselves; without them a condition that lost a
#: trial to a dropped camera would be silently one datum short, and the shortfall
#: appears nowhere because a block ends when every condition is owed nothing.
REQUEUED = frozenset(
    {
        Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK,
        Outcome.NO_FIXATION,
        Outcome.TARGET_BREAK,
        Outcome.BLINK_BREAK,
        Outcome.TRACKER_LOST,
        Outcome.FAULT,
    }
)
```

with

```python
# --- What pays a condition's debt (engine spec §16.1-16.2; engine build C2a) -------------------
#
# **Each trial structure declares its counting table** (`task.Counting`), which the scheduler is
# given (`Scheduler.counting`): "run until ten correct" and "show the array a hundred times
# regardless of accuracy" are both ordinary requirements, and a scheduler that picked one would
# silently impose an experimental design on every task in the lab. **A block may count by a table
# of its own instead** (TSK-26; `Block.counting`), and these are the named ones.

#: "Run until ten correct": only a right answer pays (`task.RIGHT`), and every other answer pays
#: nothing and is not shown again -- the block's order draws its condition as it would -- as the
#: PI ruled on 2026-08-31 ("wrong choice not" re-queued; §16.2: "Not repeated: wrong targets").
CORRECT_ONLY = Counting(
    after_stimulus=OWED,
    outcomes={outcome.value: UNPAID for outcome in ANSWERS - RIGHT},
)

#: "Show the array a hundred times regardless of accuracy": every trial whose stimulus appeared
#: pays -- an answer counted, a break or no response spent -- and one that ended before it is owed,
#: since nothing was presented.
PRESENTED = Counting(after_stimulus=SPENT)

#: Every trial pays: the flat run's "run N trials" (`taskd.Session._plan`). An answer is counted and
#: everything else spent, a trial that reached the longest trial too.
EVERY_TRIAL = Counting(
    after_stimulus=SPENT,
    outcomes={outcome.value: SPENT for outcome in Outcome if outcome not in ANSWERS},
)
```

```python
    #: purpose**: a block may count every presentation toward its quota while judging
```

`wl_xcon/scheduler.py`: replace

```python
    def owed(self, condition: str) -> int:
        """Target minus **completed**, never minus attempted.

        An aborted trial produced no datum, so it left the condition owing exactly
        what it owed before.
        """
        target = next(c.target for c in self.block.conditions if c.name == condition)
        return max(0, target - self._counts[condition].completed)

    @property
    def _judged(self) -> frozenset:
        block = self.block
        return block.criterion_over if block.criterion_over is not None else block.counts_toward
```

with

```python
    def owed(self, condition: str) -> int:
        """Target minus what paid -- the answers counted and the trials spent -- **never minus
        attempted**: an owed trial produced no datum, so it left the condition owing exactly what
        it owed before."""
        target = next(c.target for c in self.block.conditions if c.name == condition)
        counts = self._counts[condition]
        return max(0, target - counts.completed - counts.spent)

    def _class(self, outcome: Outcome | None, shown: bool) -> str:
        """What `outcome` does to its condition's count, by the block's table or the structure's
        (`task.Counting.of`). **With neither**, what every structure shares still answers -- an
        answer counted, shown or not; the four of `task.REPEATED`, a hang and a break before the
        stimulus owed -- and an outcome each structure declares for raises: no default stands in
        for a declaration (the PI, 2026-10-11), and `check.check_task` refuses a run's structure
        without one."""
        table = self.block.counting if self.block.counting is not None else self.counting
        if table is not None:
            return table.of(outcome, shown)
        if outcome in ANSWERS:
            return COUNTED
        if outcome is None or outcome in REPEATED or not shown:
            return OWED
        raise ValueError(
            f"{outcome.value} ended a trial of block {self.block.name!r}, and neither the block "
            f"nor its structure has a counting table saying what it does to the count (engine "
            f"spec §16.2)"
        )
```

```python
    def record(self, condition: str, outcome: Outcome) -> None:
        counts = self._counts[condition]
        if outcome in self.block.counts_toward:
            counts.completed += 1
            counts.correct += outcome is Outcome.CORRECT
        if outcome in self._judged:
            self._window.append(1 if outcome is Outcome.CORRECT else 0)
            if self.block.criterion is not None:
                while len(self._window) > self.block.criterion[1]:
                    self._window.popleft()
        if outcome not in self.block.counts_toward and outcome in self.block.requeue_on:
            # End of the block, not immediately: an animal must not be able to make
            # an easy condition repeat by breaking on the hard one.
            self.requeued.append(condition)
            self._queue.append(condition)
```

with

```python
    def record(self, condition: str, outcome: Outcome | None, shown: bool = True) -> str:
        """Count one trial of `condition` by its table (engine spec §16.1-16.2), and return what it
        did to the count: `COUNTED`, `SPENT`, `OWED` or `UNPAID`. `outcome` `None` is a trial that
        reached the longest trial (`task.HANG_COUNTS_AS`); `shown`, whether its stimulus had
        appeared (`task.Counting.of`). A right answer (`task.RIGHT`: correct, or a correct
        rejection) is right for the criterion and the correct count."""
        counts = self._counts[condition]
        kind = self._class(outcome, shown)
        if kind == COUNTED:
            counts.completed += 1
            counts.correct += outcome in RIGHT
        elif kind == SPENT:
            counts.spent += 1
        over = self.block.criterion_over
        if (outcome in over) if over is not None else kind == COUNTED:
            self._window.append(1 if outcome in RIGHT else 0)
            if self.block.criterion is not None:
                while len(self._window) > self.block.criterion[1]:
                    self._window.popleft()
        if kind == OWED and self.owed(condition) > 0:
            # End of the block, not immediately: an animal must not be able to make
            # an easy condition repeat by breaking on the hard one.
            self.requeued.append(condition)
            self._queue.append(condition)
        return kind
```

```python
def _appeared(trial: Trial, result) -> bool:
    """Whether a trial's stimulus had appeared (engine spec §16.2, §16.4; the engine C2a plan's
    call 4): it entered the state its structure's counting table names (`task.Counting.appears`;
    the PI, 2026-10-13, "Task names it, required"), the target's or the array's, a cued task's
    cue. A break before it is owed whatever the structure says of breaks (`task.Counting.of`),
    and the trial's row records it. A structure with no table, or one naming no state -- a test's;
    the load-time check refuses both in a task -- counts every trial as shown."""
    table = trial.counting
    return table is None or table.appears is None or table.appears in result.visited
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run them and see them pass**

Run the three files: `530 passed`. Then the whole suite: **3578 passed**.

- [ ] **Step 5: Prove they can fail**

The three defects this task fixes, each fix reverted alone:

- `task.ANSWERS` without `Outcome.CORRECT_REJECT` and `Outcome.FALSE_ALARM` (the first defect, catch trials): `test_a_catch_condition_meets_its_target_on_correct_rejections` fails (`'owed' == 'counted'`).
- `task.Counting.of`'s last line `return self.after_stimulus` → `return OWED` (the second, a target or blink break owed whatever the task says): `test_a_target_break_is_spent_or_owed_as_the_structure_declares[spent]` fails (`'owed' == 'spent'`).
- `record`'s `kind = self._class(outcome, shown)` → `kind = "none" if outcome is None else self._class(outcome, shown)` (the fourth, a hang neither counted nor repeated): `test_a_trial_at_the_longest_trial_is_owed_and_in_a_flat_run_pays` fails (`'none' == 'owed'`).

And the rest:

- `_class`'s `table = self.block.counting if self.block.counting is not None else self.counting` → `table = self.counting`: `test_a_block_can_count_only_correct_trials` fails (the block's own table unread).
- `_class`'s no-table lines in the base's order, `if outcome is None or outcome in REPEATED or not shown: return OWED` before `if outcome in ANSWERS: return COUNTED`: `test_an_outcome_each_structure_declares_is_refused_with_no_table_to_read` fails (an answer with `shown=False` owed).
- `CORRECT_ONLY`'s `{outcome.value: UNPAID for outcome in ANSWERS - RIGHT}` → `{outcome.value: OWED for outcome in ANSWERS - RIGHT}`: `test_a_wrong_answer_under_until_n_correct_pays_nothing_and_is_not_shown_again` fails (the wrong answer placed as a repeat).
- `record`'s `self._window.append(1 if outcome in RIGHT else 0)` → `self._window.append(1 if outcome is Outcome.CORRECT else 0)`: `test_a_correct_rejection_is_right_for_the_criterion_and_the_correct_count` fails; `counts.correct += outcome in RIGHT` → `counts.correct += outcome is Outcome.CORRECT`: the same test fails.
- `record` without `elif kind == SPENT: counts.spent += 1`: `test_a_block_can_count_every_presentation_regardless_of_accuracy` fails.
- `owed`'s `- counts.spent` dropped: the same test fails.
- `record`'s `... else kind == COUNTED:` (what a criterion judges) → `... else True:`: `test_a_spent_trial_pays_its_debt_and_is_no_answer_for_the_criterion` fails.
- `_plan`'s `counting=EVERY_TRIAL,` → `counting=None,`: `test_a_trial_that_reaches_no_outcome_is_still_closed` fails (the hang owed, a third trial run).
- `_appeared`'s body → `return True`: `test_each_trial_row_says_what_it_did_to_its_count_and_whether_its_stimulus_appeared` fails; its `table.appears in result.visited` → `(table.appears in result.visited or True)`: `test_whether_a_trials_stimulus_appeared_is_read_from_the_state_its_table_names` fails.

- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**

```bash
git add wl_xcon/scheduler.py wl_xcon/taskd.py wl_xcon/record.py tests/test_scheduler.py tests/test_taskd.py tests/test_service.py
git commit -m "Count by the structure's table: catch trials count, breaks follow the task, a hang is owed"
```
Body: engine spec §16.1-16.2, TSK-26 (a block's table replaces its structure's) and TSK-27; calls 2, 4, 5, 7, 14 and 15; the three defects this fixes in the counting (`RESPONDED` without correct rejections and false alarms; `REQUEUED` repeating target and blink breaks; a hang neither counted nor repeated), none reached a rig; a wrong answer under "until N correct" unpaid and not placed, as the PI ruled on 2026-08-31; a correct rejection right for the criterion and the correct count; the names removed; the red an import error by design, and each defect's fix reverted alone as a break; the count; the breaks seen failing.

---

### Task 4: Where a repeat goes, and the realized sequence

The PI's decision 1, for every block as he answered on 2026-10-12 ("Same rule, within the next k": "One rule for every block. Repeats don't pile up at the block's end."), with **k at least 3** (his answer the same day, "At least 3": "A repeat lands 2 or 3 trials later even in a two-condition block, so its position is not fixed"): a random slot within the next k trials, k the block's number of conditions and at least 3, never the very next, **chosen among the slots no other waiting repeat holds**, so none waits past its own k (the engineering review's Minor 2); the condition is not drawn otherwise while its repeat waits; when nothing else can be drawn -- it is the only condition still owed, or every other is waiting too -- or only a draw that would make a `Constrained` run too long, the earliest waiting repeat comes now. Fixes the third defect: appended to the queue, a repeat was the very next trial 8 times in 8 under `WithReplacement` and `Constrained` and 3 in 8 under `Shuffled` (seed 1, every third trial a fixation break; the scope's probe, reproduced on Task 3's code). Each trial's row records the trial it repeats, whether it came at once (§16.3: "recorded"), and the condition before it in its block (§16.4). The hang's repeat (decision 3) is pinned end to end here.

**Files:**
- Modify: `wl_xcon/scheduler.py` (`Draw` before `Block`; `Scheduler._due`, `_number`, `drawn`; `_refill`, `_drawable`, `_runs_long`, `_peek`; `next_trial`; `advance`; `record`'s repeat and `_repeat`)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/taskd.py` (`run` keeps the draw and writes it), `wl_xcon/record.py` (`SessionRecord.trial`)
- Modify (test): `tests/test_scheduler.py` (imports; `test_a_fixation_break_is_requeued_at_the_end_of_the_block` replaced; nine new), `tests/test_taskd.py` (two new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Scheduler.record`'s `OWED` (Task 3).
- Produces: `scheduler.Draw(condition: Condition, number: int, repeat_of: int | None = None, at_once: bool = False, previous: str | None = None)`, frozen; `Scheduler.drawn: Draw | None`, the last draw, set by `next_trial`, whose signature is unchanged; `Draw.number` is the trial's place in its run from 0, `taskd`'s `index`; `SessionRecord.trial(..., repeat_of: int | None = None, at_once: bool = False, previous: str | None = None)`. `Scheduler.upcoming()` still returns the ordinary queue; repeats wait apart from it.

```python
def test_a_fixation_break_is_requeued_at_the_end_of_the_block():
    """The default ruled 2026-08-31. A broken fixation is a failure to engage and
    the condition still owes a datum; end of block rather than immediately, so an
    animal cannot make an easy condition repeat by breaking on the hard one."""
    scheduler = Scheduler([_block()], seed=1)

    first = scheduler.next_trial()
    scheduler.record(first.name, Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK)

    assert first.name in scheduler.requeued
    assert scheduler.upcoming()[-1] == first.name
```

```python
def _breaking(order, seed: int = 1, every: int = 3) -> list:
    """The engine C2a plan's probe: three conditions owing five trials each, every `every`-th trial
    a fixation break, run to the block's end. Each trial's `Draw`, in order."""
    block = Block(
        name="main", conditions=[Condition(name, {}, target=5) for name in "abc"], order=order)
    scheduler = Scheduler([block], seed=seed)
    draws = []
    for _ in range(200):
        if scheduler.finished:
            return draws
        scheduler.next_trial()
        draws.append(scheduler.drawn)
        broke = scheduler.drawn.number % every == 0
        scheduler.record(scheduler.drawn.condition.name,
                         Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK if broke else Outcome.CORRECT)
    raise AssertionError("the block never ended in 200 trials")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("order", [Shuffled(), WithReplacement(), Constrained(max_run=2)],
                         ids=["shuffled", "with-replacement", "constrained"])
@pytest.mark.parametrize("seed", [1, 2, 3])
def test_a_repeat_comes_back_within_the_next_k_trials_and_never_the_very_next(order, seed):
    """§16.3, superseding 2026-08-31's "end of the block"; the PI, 2026-10-11 and 2026-10-12: a
    random slot within the next k trials, k the block's number of conditions and at least 3, never
    the very next, in every block -- unless it is the only condition still owed, then at once, and
    its draw says so. Appended to the queue, a repeat was the very next trial 8 times in 8 under
    `WithReplacement` and `Constrained` and 3 in 8 under `Shuffled` (seed 1, every third trial a
    break: the plan's third defect)."""
    draws = _breaking(order, seed=seed)

    repeats = [draw for draw in draws if draw.repeat_of is not None]
    assert len(repeats) >= 5
    for draw in repeats:
        assert draws[draw.repeat_of].condition == draw.condition
        gap = draw.number - draw.repeat_of
        assert (gap == 1) == draw.at_once
        assert 1 <= gap <= 3
    assert not any(d.at_once for d in repeats[:3]), "early on, other conditions are still owed"


def test_in_a_block_of_two_conditions_a_repeat_comes_two_or_three_trials_later():
    """The PI, 2026-10-12, "At least 3": "A repeat lands 2 or 3 trials later even in a two-condition
    block, so its position is not fixed." With k at least 2 the trial after a break was the other
    condition and the one after that the repeat, 200 seeds in 200 (the science review's D3)."""
    gaps = set()
    for seed in range(20):
        block = Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition(n, {}, target=6) for n in "ab"])
        scheduler = Scheduler([block], seed=seed)
        while not scheduler.finished:
            scheduler.next_trial()
            draw = scheduler.drawn
            if draw.repeat_of is not None and not draw.at_once:
                gaps.add(draw.number - draw.repeat_of)
            broke = draw.number % 3 == 0
            scheduler.record(draw.condition.name,
                             Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK if broke else Outcome.CORRECT)

    assert gaps == {2, 3}


def test_no_repeat_waits_past_its_k_when_breaks_come_often():
    """Each repeat takes a slot no other waiting repeat holds, so two never fall due together and
    one wait past its window (the engineering review's Minor 2: 1.17% of repeats came later than k
    with six conditions breaking half the time, when slots could be shared)."""
    for seed in range(10):
        breaks = random.Random(seed)
        block = Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition(f"c{i}", {}, target=10) for i in range(6)])
        scheduler = Scheduler([block], seed=seed)
        while not scheduler.finished:
            scheduler.next_trial()
            draw = scheduler.drawn
            if draw.repeat_of is not None:
                assert 2 <= draw.number - draw.repeat_of <= 6 or draw.at_once, (seed, draw)
            broke = breaks.random() < 0.5
            scheduler.record(draw.condition.name,
                             Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK if broke else Outcome.CORRECT)


def test_the_only_condition_still_owed_repeats_at_once_and_its_draw_says_so():
    """§16.3: "unless it is the only condition still owed, when it repeats at once, recorded"."""
    block = Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("a", {}, target=1), Condition("b", {}, target=1)])
    scheduler = Scheduler([block], seed=1)
    first = scheduler.next_trial()
    scheduler.record(first.name, Outcome.CORRECT)
    second = scheduler.next_trial()
    scheduler.record(second.name, Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK)

    again = scheduler.next_trial()

    assert again == second
    assert (scheduler.drawn.number, scheduler.drawn.repeat_of, scheduler.drawn.at_once) == (
        2, 1, True)


def test_a_waiting_repeat_holds_its_conditions_draws_and_comes_early_when_nothing_else_can():
    """A condition whose repeat is waiting is not drawn otherwise: the repeat is its next trial.
    When every condition still owed is waiting, the earliest repeat comes now, before its slot,
    though not at once. (Seed 4 gives the first repeat slot 3, so trial 2 has nothing it could
    draw but a waiting repeat.)"""
    block = Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("a", {}, target=1), Condition("b", {}, target=1),
                                        Condition("c", {}, target=0)])
    scheduler = Scheduler([block], seed=4)
    names = []
    for _ in range(2):
        names.append(scheduler.next_trial().name)
        scheduler.record(names[-1], Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK)

    third = scheduler.next_trial()

    assert sorted(names) == ["a", "b"], "each breaks once, neither drawn while its repeat waits"
    assert (third.name, scheduler.drawn.repeat_of, scheduler.drawn.at_once) == (names[0], 0, False)


def test_a_repeat_still_waiting_when_its_block_ends_stays_with_that_block():
    """A criterion can end a block while a repeat waits; the next block starts with none of the
    last one's repeats, as it starts with none of its counts."""
    first = Block(name="first", conditions=[Condition("a", {}, target=100),
                                            Condition("b", {}, target=100)], criterion=(1.0, 1))
    second = Block(name="second", conditions=[Condition("c", {}, target=1)])
    scheduler = Scheduler([first, second], seed=1)
    broke = scheduler.next_trial().name
    scheduler.record(broke, Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK)
    scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT)
    assert scheduler.finished

    scheduler.advance()

    assert [scheduler.next_trial().name for _ in range(2)] == ["c", "c"]


def test_a_constrained_block_keeps_its_runs_short_while_two_conditions_are_owed():
    """`Constrained(max_run=2)`: a repeat never makes a run of three while another condition can
    be drawn instead."""
    block = Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition(n, {}, target=20) for n in "ab"],
                  order=Constrained(max_run=2))
    scheduler = Scheduler([block], seed=3)
    runs, last = [], None
    for _ in range(200):
        if scheduler.finished:
            break
        owing = sum(scheduler.owed(name) > 0 for name in "ab")
        name = scheduler.next_trial().name
        runs.append((runs[-1][0] + 1 if name == last else 1, owing))
        last = name
        scheduler.record(name, Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK if scheduler.drawn.number % 2 == 0
                         else Outcome.CORRECT)

    assert max(length for length, owing in runs if owing == 2) <= 2


def test_a_waiting_repeat_comes_early_rather_than_let_a_constrained_run_grow():
    """`Constrained(max_run=1)`, `a` waiting for its slot and `b` the only other condition owed: a
    second `b` in a row would break the block's limit, so `a`'s repeat comes first. (Seed 8 gives
    `a`'s repeat slot 3.)"""
    block = Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("a", {}, target=5), Condition("b", {}, target=5),
                                        Condition("c", {}, target=0)], order=Constrained(max_run=1))
    scheduler = Scheduler([block], seed=8)
    drawn = []
    for outcome in (Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.CORRECT):
        drawn.append(scheduler.next_trial().name)
        scheduler.record(drawn[-1], outcome)

    assert drawn == ["a", "b", "a"]
    assert scheduler.drawn.repeat_of == 0


def test_each_draw_names_the_condition_before_it_in_its_block():
    """§16.4: "the realized sequence is recorded either way: each trial's actual predecessor,
    aborted ones included". A block's first trial has none."""
    scheduler = Scheduler(blocks=_two_blocks(), seed=1)
    seen = []
    for outcome in (Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK, Outcome.CORRECT, Outcome.CORRECT):
        seen.append((scheduler.next_trial().name, scheduler.drawn.previous))
        scheduler.record(seen[-1][0], outcome)
    scheduler.advance()
    scheduler.next_trial()

    assert seen == [("a", None), ("a", "a"), ("a", "a")]
    assert (scheduler.drawn.previous, scheduler.drawn.number) == (None, 3)
```

```python
def test_each_repeat_row_names_the_trial_it_shows_again_and_its_predecessor(tmp_path):
    """Engine spec §16.3-16.4: a repeat's row names the trial it shows again -- the same condition,
    owed -- at least two trials before it unless it came at once; every row names the condition of
    the trial before it in its block, the first none."""
    block = Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition(n, {"target_x": x}, target=8)
                                         for n, x in (("near", 5.0), ("far", 12.0), ("mid", 8.0))])
    session = _session(_spec(tmp_path, trials=400, blocks=[block]))

    session.run()

    rows = _trial_rows(session)
    by_index = {row["index"]: row for row in rows}
    repeats = [row for row in rows if row["repeat_of"] is not None]
    assert repeats, "the seed gives the block a repeat"
    for row in repeats:
        shown_again = by_index[row["repeat_of"]]
        assert (shown_again["condition"], shown_again["counting"]) == (row["condition"], "owed")
        assert (row["index"] - row["repeat_of"] == 1) == row["at_once"]
    assert [row["previous"] for row in rows] == [None] + [row["condition"] for row in rows[:-1]]
```

```python
        tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """XC-287; the PI, 2026-10-11: "it isn't a fixation break, it is a missed acquisition" --
    repeated like a fault. In a block of one condition owing one trial, the hang is owed and its
    condition, the only one still owed, comes straight back; its line still says `hang`. A hang
    neither counted nor came back before engine build C2a (the plan's fourth defect)."""

        return real(*args, **kwargs)

    monkeypatch.setattr(taskd, "run_trial", hangs_first)

            for row in _trial_rows(session)] == [("hang", "owed", None, False),
```

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Draw:
    """What `Scheduler.next_trial` drew, for the trial's row: the realized sequence (engine spec
    §16.3-16.4; engine build C2a). `number` is the trial's place in the run, from 0 -- `taskd`'s
    `index`; `repeat_of`, the number of the trial it shows again, or `None`; `at_once`, whether it
    came straight after that trial, which §16.3 has recorded; `previous`, the condition of the
    trial before it in its block, an aborted one included, or `None` for a block's first."""

    condition: Condition
    number: int
    repeat_of: int | None = None
    at_once: bool = False
    previous: str | None = None
```

```python
    def _drawable(self, condition: str) -> bool:
        """Whether an ordinary draw may give `condition`: it still owes, and no repeat of it is
        waiting for its slot -- the repeat is its next trial (engine spec §16.3)."""
        return self.owed(condition) > 0 and all(name != condition for _, _, name in self._due)

    def _runs_long(self, condition: str) -> bool:
        """Whether `condition` next would make a run longer than a `Constrained` block allows."""
        order = self.block.order
        return (
            isinstance(order, Constrained)
            and len(self._drawn) >= order.max_run
            and all(previous == condition for previous in self._drawn[-order.max_run:])
        )

    def _peek(self) -> str | None:
        """The next ordinary draw, left in the queue: entries that stopped being drawable since
        they were queued are dropped, and the queue refilled once when empty; `None` when nothing
        is drawable."""
        for _ in range(2):
            while self._queue and not self._drawable(self._queue[0]):
                self._queue.popleft()
            if self._queue:
                return self._queue[0]
            self._refill()
        return None
```

```python
    def next_trial(self) -> Condition:
        """The next trial's condition, its `Draw` left in `drawn`. **The earliest waiting repeat
        goes first** when its slot has come, when nothing else can be drawn, or when the ordinary
        draw -- which gives no condition a repeat is waiting for -- would make a run longer than a
        `Constrained` block allows (engine spec §16.3). A repeat cannot lengthen a run itself: its
        condition is not drawn while it waits, and its slot is at least two trials on."""
        number = self._number
        waiting = sorted(self._due)
        name, repeat_of = self._peek(), None
        if waiting and (waiting[0][0] <= number or name is None or self._runs_long(name)):
            due = waiting[0]
            self._due.remove(due)
            _, repeat_of, name = due
        elif name is None:
            raise IndexError(
                f"block {self.block.name!r} has no condition left to draw and its end rule is not "
                f"met"
            )
        else:
            self._queue.popleft()
        condition = next(c for c in self.block.conditions if c.name == name)
        self.drawn = Draw(
            condition,
            number,
            repeat_of=repeat_of,
            at_once=repeat_of is not None and repeat_of == number - 1,
            previous=self._drawn[-1] if self._drawn else None,
        )
        self._drawn.append(name)
        self._counts[name].attempted += 1
        self._number += 1
        return condition
```

```python
    def _repeat(self, condition: str) -> None:
        """Place the repeat of the trial just recorded (engine spec §16.3; the PI, 2026-10-11, and
        2026-10-12 for every block): **a random slot within the next k trials, k the block's
        number of conditions and at least 3, never the very next** -- so an animal cannot get an
        easy condition by breaking on a hard one, nor the same display again by breaking on it, nor
        know where a repeat comes in a block of two (the PI: "At least 3") -- **unless nothing else
        can be drawn**: it is the only condition still owed, or every other is waiting too, when
        the earliest waiting repeat comes first (`next_trial`), at once if it is this one, which
        its row records (`Draw.at_once`). **The slot is one no other waiting repeat holds**, so
        two never fall due on one trial and none waits past its window; only when every slot of
        the window is held -- more than k - 2 earlier repeats waiting -- does it share one, and a
        drum's trial, which goes before them all, can put one off by a trial. The slot is the
        block's seeded generator's, so a session's order stays reproducible."""
        number = self._number - 1
        k = max(3, len(self.block.conditions))
        held = {slot for slot, _, _ in self._due}
        free = [slot for slot in range(number + 2, number + k + 1) if slot not in held]
        slot = self._rng.choice(free) if free else number + self._rng.randint(2, k)
        self._due.append((slot, number, condition))
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Prove they can fail**

- `record`'s `if self.drumming and outcome not in RIGHT and self.owed(condition) > 0:` → `... outcome is not Outcome.CORRECT ...` (the drum ending only on `CORRECT`, the engineering review's I1): `test_a_drummed_catch_condition_finishes_on_correct_rejections` fails.
- The same line's `outcome not in RIGHT` → `outcome is Outcome.WRONG_TARGET`: `test_a_drummed_trial_comes_back_at_once_until_it_is_correct` fails (the target break not drummed).
- `next_trial`'s `drummed = self._drum is not None` → `drummed = False`: the same test fails.
- `record`'s `if judged and first_try:` → `if judged:` (a drum's repeat judged, as drafted): `test_only_a_trials_first_try_is_judged_for_the_criterion_under_drumming` fails.
- `record`'s `self._tries = 1 if first_try else self._tries + 1` → `self._tries = 1`: `test_a_drummed_trials_failed_tries_in_a_row_are_counted_for_the_consoles_warning` fails.
- `drum`'s `if scope == "off":` → `if False:`: `test_a_trainer_switches_drumming_for_a_block_or_a_run_or_off` fails (the waiting drum not dropped).
- `advance`'s `if self._switched != "run" and not self._carried:` → `if False:`: the same test fails (a block's switch carried into the next block).
- `drumming`'s `if self._switched is None and self.block.drum:` → `if self.block.drum:`: the same test fails (*off* not holding over a declaring block).
- `drum`'s `self._carried = self.finished and not self.done` → `self._carried = False`: `test_a_switch_made_as_a_block_finishes_is_for_the_next_block` fails.
- `taskd`'s `drummed=drawn.drummed,` → `drummed=False,`: `test_a_drummed_blocks_rows_say_which_trials_were_drummed` fails.
- `taskd`'s `if scheduler.drum_tries == DRUM_WARNING:` → `if False:`: `test_a_drummed_trial_that_fails_ten_tries_in_a_row_is_said_on_the_console` fails.

### Task 5: The longest trial is five minutes at the session's rate, and the console says when one is reached (XC-287)

Engine spec §6.8: "The longest trial is 5 minutes (the loop's frame cap derived from 300 s at the session's rate)". `run_trial` capped every trial at 100,000 frames whatever the rate -- about 417 s at 240 Hz, 833 s at 120 Hz -- and `taskd` passed nothing. The cap's default now derives from the frame period, so `taskd`, which passes none, and the simulator get it; a test that gives its own keeps it. What a trial reaching it counts as -- a missed acquisition, owed -- is Tasks 1, 3 and 4's (call 5). **A trial that reaches it puts a warning on the console's changes feed** (the controller's ruling on the science review's D4): which trial and condition, and what it did to the count -- shown again, or in a plain run used up as one of its trials. Every state of the reference tasks is bounded at 10 s or less, so a hang there means the loop failed or the animal never acquired for five minutes; a warning, nothing stops (warnings over refusals). **The warnings build's `longer-than-declared` warning names the trial cap** that ends a trial still waiting (`check.TRIAL_CAP`, "the trial cap, 100,000 frames (about 417 s at 240 Hz)", which its comments say this build changes, and which `tests/test_range_checks.py` pins to `run_trial`'s old default): it now names the longest trial, derived from `run.LONGEST_TRIAL_S` (`check` imports it; `run` imports only `task`, so no cycle), and the test pins the sentence to it. This task closes XC-287 with Task 11's note.

**Files:**
- Modify: `wl_xcon/run.py` (`LONGEST_TRIAL_S` and `longest_trial_frames` before `run_trial`; its `max_frames` default and docstring)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/check.py` (imports; `TRIAL_CAP` and its comment; `beyond_range`'s comment)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/taskd.py` (imports; `_hung` before `_appeared`; `run` feeds its warning)
- Modify (test): `tests/test_run.py` (imports; two tests), `tests/test_taskd.py` (one test), `tests/test_range_checks.py` (the trial cap's two asserts)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Scheduler.record`'s class and `owed` (Task 3); `Session._feed` (unchanged).
- Produces: `run.LONGEST_TRIAL_S = 300.0`; `run.longest_trial_frames(frame_period: float) -> int`, to the nearest frame; `run_trial(trial, world, frame_period, max_frames: int | None = None, ...)`, `None` meaning `longest_trial_frames(frame_period)`; `check.TRIAL_CAP = "the longest trial (5 minutes)"`, from `LONGEST_TRIAL_S`; `taskd._hung(index, condition, counted, owed) -> str`, the warning's sentence; a `("warning", None, at, said)` row on `Session.controls` for each hang.

```python
def test_the_longest_trial_is_five_minutes_at_the_sessions_rate():
    """Engine spec §6.8: "the loop's frame cap derived from 300 s at the session's rate" (XC-287).
    It was 100,000 frames whatever the rate, about 417 s at 240 Hz."""
    assert LONGEST_TRIAL_S == 300.0
    assert longest_trial_frames(1 / 240) == 72_000
    assert longest_trial_frames(1 / 120) == 36_000
    assert longest_trial_frames(0.7) == 429, "to the nearest frame: 428.57"


@pytest.mark.parametrize("frame_period", [1.0, 0.5])
def test_a_trial_that_reaches_the_longest_trial_ends_there_with_no_outcome(frame_period):
    """A wait the five minutes cut short: the trial ends at the cap, a hang, whatever its own
    bound. (Slow frames keep the test to a few hundred.)"""
    trial = Trial(start="wait", states=[State("wait", go=[On(After(400.0), Outcome.CORRECT)])])

    result = run_trial(trial, world=Quiet(), frame_period=frame_period)

    assert result.outcome is None
    assert result.frames == round(300 / frame_period)
```

```python
#: **The longest trial** (engine spec §6.8), in seconds: a trial that reaches it without an
#: outcome ends there as a hang (`Result.outcome` `None`), which `taskd` records as `hang` and
#: counts as a missed acquisition (`task.HANG_COUNTS_AS`; the PI, 2026-10-11, XC-287). Five
#: minutes; longer presentations are the continuous mode's (§13.6).
LONGEST_TRIAL_S = 300.0


def longest_trial_frames(frame_period: float) -> int:
    """`LONGEST_TRIAL_S` in whole frames at `frame_period` (engine spec §6.8: "the loop's frame cap
    derived from 300 s at the session's rate"), to the nearest frame: 72,000 at 240 Hz and 36,000
    at 120 Hz."""
    return round(LONGEST_TRIAL_S / frame_period)


def run_trial(
    trial: Trial,
    world: World,
    frame_period: float,
    max_frames: int | None = None,
```

```python
def _hung(index: int, condition: str, counted: str, owed: int) -> str:
    """What the console's changes feed says of a trial that reached the longest trial (engine spec
    §6.8; the engine C2a plan, its science review's D4): which trial of which condition, and what
    it did to the count -- a missed acquisition (the PI, 2026-10-11), shown again unless the table
    says otherwise or a repeat limit forgave the condition's debt."""
    did = {
        OWED: "shown again" if owed else "owed, its condition's remaining debt forgiven",
        SPENT: "used up as one of the run's trials",
        COUNTED: "counted as an answer",
        UNPAID: "left unpaid",
    }[counted]
    return (
        f"trial {index} ({condition}) reached the longest trial, {LONGEST_TRIAL_S / 60:g} minutes, "
        f"with no outcome: recorded as a hang, a missed acquisition, and {did}"
    )
```

### Task 6: Drumming

The PI's decision 2: drumming, "a training tool ... where a trial is repeated until correct", its own option, the same trial back **immediately** until correct, "switched on per block or run by the trainer", recorded. A block declares it (`Block.drum`, which build C2b's plans will write); the scheduler's `drum` switches it for the rest of a block or a run, or off (the console sends it in Task 8). **Every outcome but a right one** -- a correct answer, or a correct rejection, `task.RIGHT` (the controller's ruling on the engineering review's I1: a drummed catch condition otherwise never finished) -- brings the same condition straight back and pays nothing, whatever the table says; a drum's repeat does not count toward the repeat limit (call 7). **Only a trial's first try is judged for a criterion** (the PI, 2026-10-12, "Leave drummed repeats out": "Only each trial's first try is judged for the criterion. The repeats are still recorded as what they were."). **After 10 failed tries in a row of one drummed trial the console says so**, and drumming goes on (the PI, 2026-10-12, "After 10 in a row": "A warning on the console after 10 failed tries in a row"). A switch made once a block's end rule is met is for the next block, since the loop drains commands before it advances (the engineering review's Minor 10). Each trial's row says whether it is a drum's repeat and whether drumming was on.

**Files:**
- Modify: `wl_xcon/scheduler.py` (`DRUM_SCOPES` and `DRUM_WARNING` before `Draw`; `Draw.drummed`, `drumming`; `Block.drum`; `Scheduler._drum`, `_switched`, `_carried`, `_tries`; `advance`, `drumming`, `drum_tries`, `drum`; `next_trial`; `record`)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/taskd.py` (imports; `run`'s trial row and its warning), `wl_xcon/record.py` (`SessionRecord.trial`)
- Modify (test): `tests/test_scheduler.py` (six new), `tests/test_taskd.py` (two new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Draw`, `Scheduler.drawn` (Task 4); `task.RIGHT` (Task 1).
- Produces: `scheduler.DRUM_SCOPES = ("block", "run", "off")`; `scheduler.DRUM_WARNING = 10`; `Block.drum: bool = False`; `Scheduler.drumming -> str | None` (`"block"`, `"run"` or `None`); `Scheduler.drum(scope: str) -> str`, the name of the block the switch is for, raising `ValueError` on a scope not in `DRUM_SCOPES`; `Scheduler.drum_tries -> int`; `Draw.drummed: bool = False`, `Draw.drumming: str | None = None`; `SessionRecord.trial(..., drummed: bool = False, drumming: str | None = None)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`tests/test_scheduler.py`: insert

```python
# --- drumming (the PI, 2026-10-11) -----------------------------------------------


def _drummed(**block) -> Scheduler:
    """Three conditions owing two trials each, by a table that spends breaks after the stimulus."""
    return Scheduler(
        [Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition(n, {}, target=2) for n in "abc"], **block)],
        seed=1, counting=Counting(after_stimulus=SPENT),
    )


def test_a_drummed_trial_comes_back_at_once_until_it_is_correct():
    """"A training tool ... where a trial is repeated until correct": a wrong answer, which counts
    elsewhere, and a break, which this table spends, both pay nothing and bring the same condition
    straight back; the correct one pays. Each repeat's draw says it was drummed."""
    scheduler = _drummed(drum=True)
    first = scheduler.next_trial()
    draws = []
    for outcome in (Outcome.WRONG_TARGET, Outcome.TARGET_BREAK, Outcome.CORRECT):
        assert scheduler.record(scheduler.drawn.condition.name, outcome) == (
            COUNTED if outcome is Outcome.CORRECT else OWED)
        if outcome is not Outcome.CORRECT:
            scheduler.next_trial()
            draws.append(scheduler.drawn)

    assert [d.condition for d in draws] == [first, first]
    assert [(d.repeat_of, d.at_once, d.drummed, d.drumming) for d in draws] == [
        (0, True, True, "block"), (1, True, True, "block")]
    assert scheduler.owed(first.name) == 1 and scheduler.counts(first.name).spent == 0


def test_only_a_trials_first_try_is_judged_for_the_criterion_under_drumming():
    """The PI, 2026-10-12, "Leave drummed repeats out": "Only each trial's first try is judged for
    the criterion. The repeats are still recorded as what they were." Judging the repeats let an
    animal that always picks one side meet 60% over 30 at 50% true accuracy (the science review's
    D2): lose-shift solves a correction trial."""
    scheduler = Scheduler(
        [Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("a", {}, target=100)], criterion=(0.5, 2),
               drum=True)], seed=1)
    for outcome in (Outcome.WRONG_TARGET, Outcome.CORRECT):
        scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, outcome)

    assert scheduler.drawn.drummed and not scheduler.finished, "one first try, not two"
    scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT)
    assert scheduler.finished, "two first tries, half of them right"


def test_a_drummed_catch_condition_finishes_on_correct_rejections():
    """The controller's ruling on the engineering review's I1: a drum ends on a right answer, and a
    catch trial's right answer is a correct rejection; drummed, the condition owed 2 after 50 of
    them and its block never ended."""
    block = Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("catch", {}, target=2)], drum=True)
    scheduler = Scheduler([block], seed=1,
                          counting=Counting(after_stimulus=OWED, withholding=True))
    for _ in range(2):
        assert scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT_REJECT) == COUNTED

    assert scheduler.finished and not scheduler.drawn.drummed


def test_a_drummed_trials_failed_tries_in_a_row_are_counted_for_the_consoles_warning():
    """The PI, 2026-10-12, "After 10 in a row": the first try and each drum's repeat that fails are
    tries in a row of one trial; a right answer ends the run of them."""
    from wl_xcon.scheduler import DRUM_WARNING

    scheduler = Scheduler([Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("a", {}, target=2)], drum=True)],
                          seed=1)
    tries = []
    for _ in range(DRUM_WARNING):
        scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET)
        tries.append(scheduler.drum_tries)
    scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT)

    assert tries == list(range(1, DRUM_WARNING + 1)) and DRUM_WARNING == 10
    assert scheduler.drum_tries == 0


def test_a_trainer_switches_drumming_for_a_block_or_a_run_or_off():
    """The PI, 2026-10-11: "switched on per block or run by the trainer". On for the block ends with
    it; on for the run carries on; off holds to the block's end, over a block that declares it, and
    drops a drum waiting to come back."""
    blocks = [Block(name=n, conditions=[Condition(n.lower(), {}, target=1)], drum=declared)
              for n, declared in (("A", False), ("B", False), ("C", True), ("D", False))]
    scheduler = Scheduler(blocks, seed=1)
    seen = [scheduler.drumming]
    scheduler.drum("block")
    seen.append(scheduler.drumming)
    scheduler.advance()
    seen.append(scheduler.drumming)
    scheduler.advance()
    seen.append(scheduler.drumming)
    scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET)
    scheduler.drum("off")
    seen.append(scheduler.drumming)
    seen.append(scheduler.next_trial().name == "c" and scheduler.drawn.drummed)
    scheduler.drum("run")
    scheduler.record("c", Outcome.CORRECT)
    scheduler.advance()
    seen.append(scheduler.drumming)

    assert seen == [None, "block", None, "block", None, False, "run"]
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="drumming is switched block, run, off"):
        scheduler.drum("on")


def test_a_switch_made_as_a_block_finishes_is_for_the_next_block():
    """The loop drains a trainer's commands before it advances, so a switch that arrives as a block
    finishes is for the next block, and says which (the engineering review's Minor 10: it was
    lost, its control row naming the block that had ended)."""
    blocks = [Block(name=n, conditions=[Condition(n.lower(), {}, target=1)]) for n in "AB"]
    scheduler = Scheduler(blocks, seed=1)
    scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.CORRECT)
    assert scheduler.finished

    said = scheduler.drum("block")
    scheduler.advance()

    assert (said, scheduler.block.name, scheduler.drumming) == ("B", "B", "block")
```

```python
def test_a_drummed_blocks_rows_say_which_trials_were_drummed(tmp_path):
    """The PI, 2026-10-11: drumming is recorded. A block that declares it repeats every trial that
    is not correct at once; each such row is followed by its drum, which names it, and every row of
    the block says drumming was on."""
    block = Block(name="drill", conditions=[Condition("c", {}, target=20)], drum=True)
    session = _session(_spec(tmp_path, trials=400, blocks=[block]))

    session.run()

    rows = _trial_rows(session)
    assert {row["drumming"] for row in rows} == {"block"}
    assert [row["outcome"] for row in rows].count("correct") == 20
    for before, row in zip(rows, rows[1:]):
        assert row["drummed"] == (before["outcome"] != "correct")
        if row["drummed"]:
            assert (row["repeat_of"], row["at_once"]) == (before["index"], True)
    assert any(row["drummed"] for row in rows), "the seed gives the block a trial not correct"


def test_a_drummed_trial_that_fails_ten_tries_in_a_row_is_said_on_the_console(
        tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """The PI, 2026-10-12, "After 10 in a row": "A warning on the console after 10 failed tries in a
    row" of one drummed trial, naming it; drumming goes on, and every try is recorded as it was."""
    from wl_xcon import taskd

    real, calls = taskd.run_trial, []

    def wrong_ten_times(*args, **kwargs):
        calls.append(None)
        result = real(*args, **kwargs)
        if len(calls) > 10:
            return result
        return dataclasses.replace(result, outcome=Outcome.WRONG_TARGET)

    monkeypatch.setattr(taskd, "run_trial", wrong_ten_times)
    block = Block(name="drill", conditions=[Condition("c", {}, target=2)], drum=True)
    session = _session(_spec(tmp_path, trials=400, blocks=[block]))

    session.run()

    rows = _trial_rows(session)
    warned = [said for kind, by, _, said in session.controls if kind == "warning" and by is None]
    assert warned == ["c has failed 10 tries in a row under drumming, the last trial 9; drumming "
                      "goes on: clear one moves past it, stop drumming ends it"]
    assert [row["outcome"] for row in rows[:10]] == ["wrong_target"] * 10
    assert [row["drummed"] for row in rows[:11]] == [False] + [True] * 10
    assert session.stop_kind == "completed"
```

```python
    @property
    def drumming(self) -> str | None:
        """Whether trials are drummed now (`Block.drum`): `"run"` when a trainer switched it on for
        the run, `"block"` when for this block or the block declares it, `None` when it is off --
        a trainer's *off* holds to the block's end, a declaring block's included."""
        if self._switched in ("run", "block"):
            return self._switched
        if self._switched is None and self.block.drum:
            return "block"
        return None

    @property
    def drum_tries(self) -> int:
        """Failed tries in a row of the trial being drummed, its first try included, or 0 when no
        drum's trial waits: at `DRUM_WARNING` the console warns, and drumming goes on (the PI,
        2026-10-12)."""
        return self._tries if self._drum is not None else 0

    def drum(self, scope: str) -> str:
        """Switch drumming, as a trainer does from the console (the PI, 2026-10-11): `"block"` on
        until this block ends, `"run"` on until the run ends, `"off"` off until this block ends.
        Off drops a drum's trial waiting to come back; its condition stays owed and is drawn as
        any other. **Returns the name of the block it is for**: this one, or the next when this
        block's end rule is already met -- the loop drains commands before it advances, so a
        switch made then is the next block's (the engineering review's Minor 10)."""
        if scope not in DRUM_SCOPES:
            raise ValueError(f"drumming is switched {', '.join(DRUM_SCOPES)}, not {scope!r}")
        self._switched = scope
        if scope == "off":
            self._drum = None
        self._carried = self.finished and not self.done
        return self.blocks[self._index + 1].name if self._carried else self.block.name
```

Run the two files: `304 passed`. Then the whole suite: **3596 passed**.

- [ ] **Step 5: Prove they can fail**

- `record`'s `outcome is not Outcome.CORRECT` (in the drumming branch) → `outcome is Outcome.WRONG_TARGET`: `test_a_drummed_trial_comes_back_at_once_until_it_is_correct` fails (the target break not drummed).
- `next_trial`'s `drummed = self._drum is not None` → `drummed = False`: the same test fails.
- `record`'s judging line `if (outcome in over) if over is not None else kind == COUNTED:` → `if False:`: `test_a_drummed_wrong_answer_is_still_judged_for_the_criterion` fails.
- `drum`'s `if scope == "off":` → `if False:`: `test_a_trainer_switches_drumming_for_a_block_or_a_run_or_off` fails (the waiting drum not dropped).
- `advance`'s `if self._switched != "run":` → `if False:`: the same test fails (a block's switch carried into the next block).
- `drumming`'s `if self._switched is None and self.block.drum:` → `if self.block.drum:`: the same test fails (*off* not holding over a declaring block).
- `taskd`'s `drummed=drawn.drummed,` → `drummed=False,`: `test_a_drummed_blocks_rows_say_which_trials_were_drummed` fails.

- [ ] **Step 6: Commit**

```bash
git add wl_xcon/scheduler.py wl_xcon/taskd.py wl_xcon/record.py tests/test_scheduler.py tests/test_taskd.py
git commit -m "Drum a trial back at once until it is correct, by a block's declaration or a trainer's switch"
```
Body: the PI's decision 2 and his words; his answers of 2026-10-12 (only a trial's first try judged; a warning after 10 failed tries in a row, drumming going on); a drum ends on a right answer, a correct rejection too (the engineering review's I1); a switch made at a block's end kept for the next block (its Minor 10); call 7; recorded in each trial's row here, the switch's own record with the console (Task 8), and build C3's change log after (XC-363); the count; the breaks seen failing.

---

### Task 7: The repeat limit and the shortfall it leaves

Engine spec §16.6: "past N repeats in a block, the condition's remaining debt is forgiven, its shortfall recorded, shown and flagged as possible avoidance", **as the PI read it on 2026-10-12**: a task's repeat limit counts "Tries in a row of one trial" -- "The condition is moved past after 3 failed repeats in a row, and the count resets once it is done." The limit is the table's (`Counting.cap`, Task 1), none unless a task sets one (the PI's decision 5); the scheduler counts each condition's failed repeats in a row, resetting the count when the condition pays, and past the limit forgives what the condition still owes as a `Shortfall`, which the run's end row records (call 10) and the console shows (Task 9). **Only the animal's outcomes are flagged as possible avoidance** (the controller's ruling on the engineering review's M4): a limit reached with tries the rig lost among them -- a lost tracker or a fault -- is a shortfall named for the rig, never flagged (call 9). A wrong answer left unpaid ("until N correct") is not placed as a repeat, so it never feeds the limit, and a drum's repeat does not either (call 7). The person's clear (Task 8) forgives through the same `_forgive`.

**Files:**
- Modify: `wl_xcon/scheduler.py` (imports; `CAP`, `RIG` and `CLEARED`; `Counts.repeats`, `in_a_row`, `rig`, `forgiven`; `Shortfall`; `Scheduler.shortfalls`; `owed`; `record`'s limit and `_forgive`)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/taskd.py` (imports; the run's end row)
- Modify (test): `tests/test_scheduler.py` (imports; six new), `tests/test_taskd.py` (one new; its names imported since Task 3)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Counting.cap` (Task 1); `Scheduler._repeat`, `_due`, `_drum` (Tasks 4, 6); `task.Family` (unchanged).
- Produces: `scheduler.CAP = "cap"`, `RIG = "rig"`, `CLEARED = "cleared"`; `scheduler.Shortfall(block: str, condition: str, trials: int, because: str, after_trial: int, by: object = None)`, frozen, `possible_avoidance -> bool` (`because == CAP`); `Counts.repeats`, `in_a_row`, `rig`, `forgiven`; `Scheduler.shortfalls: list[Shortfall]`, the run's, every block's; `Scheduler._forgive(condition, trials, because, by=None)`; the run's end row's `shortfalls`, a list of `{"block", "condition", "trials", "because", "possible_avoidance", "after_trial", "by"}`.

```python
# --- the repeat cap (engine spec §16.6) ----------------------------------------------


def _breaking_on(cap, breaks: int, outcomes=(Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK,),
                 target: int = 3) -> Scheduler:
    """One condition owing `target` trials, its table's limit `cap`, its trials ending as
    `outcomes` say in turn, `breaks` of them."""
    scheduler = Scheduler([Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("a", {}, target=target)])],
                          seed=1, counting=Counting(after_stimulus=OWED, cap=cap))
    for n in range(breaks):
        scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, outcomes[n % len(outcomes)])
    return scheduler


def test_past_its_cap_a_conditions_debt_is_forgiven_and_flagged_as_possible_avoidance():
    """§16.6: "past N repeats in a block, the condition's remaining debt is forgiven, its shortfall
    recorded, shown and flagged as possible avoidance". Two repeats allowed: the third break in a
    row forgives the three trials still owed, and the block can end."""
    scheduler = _breaking_on(cap=2, breaks=3)

    assert scheduler.requeued == ["a", "a"] and scheduler.counts("a").repeats == 2
    assert scheduler.shortfalls == [Shortfall("b", "a", 3, CAP, 2)]
    assert scheduler.shortfalls[0].possible_avoidance
    assert scheduler.owed("a") == 0 and scheduler.finished


def test_with_no_cap_a_condition_is_repeated_until_done():
    """The PI, 2026-10-11: with no cap set, "no limit (repeat until done)"; the console's clear
    moves past one by hand."""
    scheduler = _breaking_on(cap=None, breaks=50)

    assert scheduler.counts("a").repeats == 50 and scheduler.shortfalls == []
    assert scheduler.owed("a") == 3 and not scheduler.finished


def test_a_cap_of_zero_forgives_at_the_first_owed_trial():
    """A cap of 0: no repeat at all; the first owed trial forgives the debt."""
    scheduler = _breaking_on(cap=0, breaks=1)

    assert scheduler.requeued == [] and [s.trials for s in scheduler.shortfalls] == [3]


def test_the_repeat_limit_counts_failed_repeats_in_a_row_and_resets_when_the_condition_pays():
    """The PI, 2026-10-12: "Tries in a row of one trial" -- "The condition is moved past after 3
    failed repeats in a row, and the count resets once it is done." Counted over the block, a limit
    of 2 flagged 73% of conditions at an ordinary 20% break rate with a cap of 3 (the science
    review's O1); here two breaks, a correct trial, two breaks and a correct trial are four
    repeats and no shortfall, and only a third failure in a row forgives."""
    run = (Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK, Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK, Outcome.CORRECT)
    paid = _breaking_on(cap=2, breaks=6, outcomes=run, target=5)
    moved_past = _breaking_on(cap=2, breaks=6, outcomes=run + run[:2] + run[:1], target=5)

    assert (paid.counts("a").repeats, paid.shortfalls, paid.owed("a")) == (4, [], 3)
    assert moved_past.shortfalls == [Shortfall("b", "a", 4, CAP, 5)]


def test_a_shortfall_the_rig_made_is_named_for_the_rig_and_not_flagged_as_avoidance():
    """The controller's ruling on the engineering review's M4: only the animal's outcomes are
    flagged as possible avoidance. A limit reached with tries a lost tracker or a fault ended is
    named for the rig, and a camera that keeps dropping a condition is not called the animal's."""
    rig = _breaking_on(cap=1, breaks=2, outcomes=(Outcome.TRACKER_LOST,))
    mixed = _breaking_on(cap=1, breaks=2, outcomes=(Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK, Outcome.FAULT))
    animal = _breaking_on(cap=1, breaks=2, outcomes=(Outcome.FIXATION_BREAK, Outcome.NO_FIXATION))

    assert [(s.because, s.possible_avoidance) for s in rig.shortfalls] == [(RIG, False)]
    assert [(s.because, s.possible_avoidance) for s in mixed.shortfalls] == [(RIG, False)]
    assert [(s.because, s.possible_avoidance) for s in animal.shortfalls] == [(CAP, True)]


def test_a_wrong_answer_left_unpaid_never_feeds_the_repeat_limit():
    """The controller's ruling on the engineering review's I3: under "until N correct" a wrong
    answer is not placed as a repeat, so a condition the animal keeps getting wrong is neither
    forgiven nor flagged as avoidance."""
    table = Counting(after_stimulus=OWED, outcomes={"wrong_target": UNPAID}, cap=0)
    scheduler = Scheduler([Block(name="b", conditions=[Condition("a", {}, target=2)])], seed=1,
                          counting=table)
    for _ in range(5):
        scheduler.record(scheduler.next_trial().name, Outcome.WRONG_TARGET)

    assert (scheduler.shortfalls, scheduler.requeued, scheduler.owed("a")) == ([], [], 2)
```

```python
    """One condition's counters in its block (TSK-28): trials drawn; answers counted (`completed`),
    the correct among them; trials spent, used up without an answer (engine spec §16.2); times it
    was repeated (`repeats`; a drum's are not); its failed repeats in a row since it last paid
    (`in_a_row`, which the repeat limit counts: §16.6 as the PI read it on 2026-10-12) and how
    many of those failed tries the rig lost (`rig`); and trials of its debt forgiven, by the limit
    or by a person (`forgiven`)."""

    attempted: int = 0
    completed: int = 0
    correct: int = 0
    spent: int = 0
    repeats: int = 0
    in_a_row: int = 0
    rig: int = 0

@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Shortfall:
    """Trials of a condition's debt forgiven in a block, recorded and shown (engine spec §16.6):
    `because` its repeat limit was reached by the animal's own failures (`CAP`), which is **flagged
    as possible avoidance**; or reached with tries the rig lost among them (`RIG`), named so and
    never flagged; or a person cleared them from the console (`CLEARED`, by `by`). `after_trial`,
    the number of the trial it happened after."""

    block: str
    condition: str
    trials: int
    because: str
    after_trial: int
    by: object = None

    @property
    def possible_avoidance(self) -> bool:
        """§16.6: a condition the animal itself failed past its limit is flagged as possible
        avoidance; one the rig failed on is not (the engine C2a plan's engineering review, M4)."""
        return self.because == CAP
```

```python
        if kind == COUNTED:
            counts.completed += 1
```

```python
        if kind in (COUNTED, SPENT):
            # The condition paid, so its failed repeats in a row start again (the PI, 2026-10-12:
            # "the count resets once it is done").

            cap = None if table is None else table.cap
            if cap is not None and counts.in_a_row >= cap:
                # **Past its limit, the condition's remaining debt is forgiven** (§16.6; the PI,
                # 2026-10-12: "moved past after 3 failed repeats in a row"), its shortfall
                # recorded -- flagged as possible avoidance only when the animal failed each try.
                self._forgive(condition, self.owed(condition), RIG if counts.rig else CAP)
            else:
                counts.repeats += 1
                counts.in_a_row += 1
                self.requeued.append(condition)
                self._repeat(condition)
        return kind

    def _forgive(self, condition: str, trials: int, because: str, by: object = None) -> None:
        """Forgive `trials` of `condition`'s debt in this block (§16.6): no repeat of it waits any
        longer, nor a drum's trial, and the shortfall is the run's record."""
        self._counts[condition].forgiven += trials
        self._due = [due for due in self._due if due[2] != condition]
        if self._drum is not None and self._drum[0] == condition:
            self._drum = None
        self.shortfalls.append(
            Shortfall(self.block.name, condition, trials, because, self._number - 1, by))
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run them and see them pass**

Run the two files: `321 passed`. Then the whole suite: **3616 passed**.

- [ ] **Step 5: Prove they can fail**

- `record`'s `if cap is not None and counts.in_a_row >= cap:` → `if False:`: `test_past_its_cap_a_conditions_debt_is_forgiven_and_flagged_as_possible_avoidance` fails.
- `record` without `counts.in_a_row += 1`: the same test fails (the limit never reached).
- `owed`'s `- counts.forgiven` dropped: the same test fails (the debt still owed).
- `record`'s `counts.in_a_row = counts.rig = 0` → `pass` (the count over the block, as drafted): `test_the_repeat_limit_counts_failed_repeats_in_a_row_and_resets_when_the_condition_pays` fails.
- `record`'s `RIG if counts.rig else CAP` → `CAP` (rig failures flagged as avoidance, the engineering review's M4): `test_a_shortfall_the_rig_made_is_named_for_the_rig_and_not_flagged_as_avoidance` fails; `counts.rig += outcome is not None and outcome.family is Family.RIG` → `counts.rig += False`: the same test fails.
- `record`'s `if kind == OWED and self.owed(condition) > 0:` → `if kind in (OWED, UNPAID) and self.owed(condition) > 0:`: `test_a_wrong_answer_left_unpaid_never_feeds_the_repeat_limit` fails.
- The end row's `"possible_avoidance": short.possible_avoidance,` → `"possible_avoidance": False,`: `test_a_runs_end_row_records_each_shortfall_its_cap_forgave` fails.

```bash
git add wl_xcon/scheduler.py wl_xcon/taskd.py tests/test_scheduler.py tests/test_taskd.py
git commit -m "Forgive a condition's debt past its repeat cap, recorded and flagged as possible avoidance"
```

Body: §16.6, as the PI read it on 2026-10-12 (failed repeats in a row of one trial, the count reset once the condition pays); his decision 5 (no limit unless a task sets one); only the animal's outcomes flagged as possible avoidance, a limit reached with the rig's failures among the tries named for the rig (the engineering review's M4); a wrong answer left unpaid never feeding the limit (its I3); calls 9 and 10 (the end row, since wl-preproc reads only start and end rows); the red an import error by design; the count; the breaks seen failing.

---

### Task 8: Clearing a trial and switching drumming from the console

The PI's decision 5: "there should be some way in the console to manually advance beyond or clear a specific trial in the count", recorded; and decision 2's switch, "by the trainer". Two commands on the wire, `ClearOwed` and `Drum`, decoded by the wire's rules; in the session, **two branches in `Session._command`, beside the pause and outside its two welfare-listed parts** (its `held` pass-through and its one `except` line), so a command drained while paused (`_hold`) reaches them too. A clear forgives one trial of the condition's debt in the open block, or **the rest of it** (the controller's ruling on the science review's D5: a refused condition owing 20 needed 20 clicks while it kept returning) (`Scheduler.clear`, call 8), refused with a sentence where there is nothing to clear; each is a `controls.jsonl` row with who sent it, and a clear is a shortfall on the run's end row. A drumming switch drained as a block ends is the next block's, and its row says so (Task 6; the engineering review's Minor 10). Build C3's change log takes both over with their reasons (XC-363).

**Files:**
- Modify: `wl_xcon/link.py` (docstring; imports; `ClearOwed`, `Drum`; `Command`; `_encode_command`; `_command_from`)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/scheduler.py` (`Scheduler.clear`, before `drum`)
- Modify: `wl_xcon/taskd.py` (`Session._command`'s two branches; `Session._clear` and `_drum`, before `_ends`)
- Modify (test): `tests/test_link.py` (imports; two new), `tests/test_scheduler.py` (imports; three new), `tests/test_taskd.py` (imports; `_Drained`, `_near_far`; five new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Scheduler._forgive`, `CLEARED`, `Shortfall` (Task 7); `Scheduler.drum`, `drumming`, `DRUM_SCOPES` (Task 6); `Session._control`, `_refuse` (unchanged).
- Produces: `link.ClearOwed(condition: str, by: Actor, rest: bool = False)`, `KIND = "clear"`, wire `{"kind": "clear", "by", "condition", "rest"}`, `rest` true or false or refused; `link.Drum(scope: str, by: Actor)`, `KIND = "drum"`, wire `{"kind": "drum", "by", "scope"}`, `scope` one of `DRUM_SCOPES` or refused (`CommandRefused`); `Scheduler.clear(condition: str, by: object = None, rest: bool = False) -> str | None`, the refusal's sentence or `None`; `Session._clear(command, index)`, `Session._drum(command, index)`; `controls.jsonl` rows of kind `clear` (`condition`, `block`, `rest`, `cleared`, `owed`) and `drum` (`scope`, `block`, `was`, `now`).

```python
def test_clearing_a_trial_and_switching_drumming_survive_the_wire_with_who_sent_them(command):
    """The PI, 2026-10-11: a console control to clear a trial from the count, and drumming switched
```
